        Idea:
Implement Phase 4 (Python DSL + verify helpers + author check/describe/new) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md sections 10 and 14. Phase 4 scope: ship `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL as the only committed authoring representation. Authors write `<pack>/<orch>.py` using decorators/helpers like `@orchestrator`, `code()`, `attested()`, `nested()`. Step references are typed (e.g., `step.notes` resolves a produces handle, typo-checked at definition). The DSL emits a validated definition equivalent to the JSON manifest schema. Add `artagents author compile` to write the JSON manifest into `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` (gitignored); the runtime hashes/pins this artifact. Add `artagents author check` (sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested non-trivial produces have semantic checks, code argv to `artagents orchestrators run` is rejected). Add `artagents author describe` (pretty-prints DAG: step kinds, produces, requires, max_iterations, repeat config, estimated cost ceiling if cost hints present). Add `artagents author new <pack>.<name>` (scaffolds Python file + fixtures/<name>/ + golden/<name>.events.jsonl from a template). The verify helper library (artagents/verify/) is already shipped from Phase 3 — re-use file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of as DSL helpers. Phases 1 (kernel), 2 (three step kinds), 3 (produces + repeat) are committed and shipped — build on top. Do NOT implement new lifecycle verbs (Phase 5). Keep additive: existing JSON manifests and python/command runtimes still work. Tests: typed step reference catches typos at definition; compile produces a valid manifest in build/; check validates references and rejects sentinel-only attested produces; describe pretty-prints expected DAG; new scaffolds correct structure; existing JSON manifests still load and run.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

        Idea:
Implement Phase 4 (Python DSL + verify helpers + author check/describe/new) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md sections 10 and 14. Phase 4 scope: ship `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL as the only committed authoring representation. Authors write `<pack>/<orch>.py` using decorators/helpers like `@orchestrator`, `code()`, `attested()`, `nested()`. Step references are typed (e.g., `step.notes` resolves a produces handle, typo-checked at definition). The DSL emits a validated definition equivalent to the JSON manifest schema. Add `artagents author compile` to write the JSON manifest into `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` (gitignored); the runtime hashes/pins this artifact. Add `artagents author check` (sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested non-trivial produces have semantic checks, code argv to `artagents orchestrators run` is rejected). Add `artagents author describe` (pretty-prints DAG: step kinds, produces, requires, max_iterations, repeat config, estimated cost ceiling if cost hints present). Add `artagents author new <pack>.<name>` (scaffolds Python file + fixtures/<name>/ + golden/<name>.events.jsonl from a template). The verify helper library (artagents/verify/) is already shipped from Phase 3 — re-use file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of as DSL helpers. Phases 1 (kernel), 2 (three step kinds), 3 (produces + repeat) are committed and shipped — build on top. Do NOT implement new lifecycle verbs (Phase 5). Keep additive: existing JSON manifests and python/command runtimes still work. Tests: typed step reference catches typos at definition; compile produces a valid manifest in build/; check validates references and rejects sentinel-only attested produces; describe pretty-prints expected DAG; new scaffolds correct structure; existing JSON manifests still load and run.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

      "description": "Implement DSL builder classes and step factories in `artagents/orchestrate/dsl.py`. Define `OrchestrateDefinitionError`, `_ProducesHandle(step_id, name)` whose `__str__` returns `\"<step_id>.produces.<name>\"`, `_StepHandle` that carries `id/kind/command|argv|plan/produces/repeat/instructions/ack` and uses `__getattr__` to look up produces names raising `OrchestrateDefinitionError` with the declared keys list on miss, `_RepeatUntilSpec`, `_RepeatForEachSpec`, and `_PlanBuilder`. Add factories `code(step_id, *, argv, produces=None, repeat=None, cost_hint_usd=None)`, `attested(step_id, *, command, instructions, ack, produces=None, repeat=None, cost_hint_usd=None)`, `nested(step_id, *, plan, produces=None, repeat=None, cost_hint_usd=None)`, plus `repeat_until(condition, *, max_iterations, on_exhaust, quorum_n=None)` and `repeat_for_each(items=None, *, from_=None)`. Add `plan(plan_id, steps)` and `@orchestrator(plan_id)` decorator that accepts either a function returning a `_PlanBuilder` or a list of steps (auto-wrapped via `plan(...)`). Required guards (FLAGs 002/003/004/005/006): (a) reject produces names that collide with `_StepHandle` reserved attribute names (`id`, `kind`, `command`, `argv`, `plan`, `produces`, `repeat`, `instructions`, `ack`) at construction; (b) reject `code` argv that resolves to `artagents orchestrators run` by re-using `artagents.core.task.plan._strip_artagents_prefix` and the same matching logic from `_reject_orchestrators_run`; (c) reject `attested` produces values whose check is sentinel-only (e.g. bare `file_nonempty()`) at construction; (d) accept `argv` elements as `str | _ProducesHandle` and stringify handles before `shlex.join`; (e) raise on duplicate sibling step ids inside `_PlanBuilder`. Implement `_PlanBuilder.to_dict()` returning the JSON shape that `artagents.core.task.plan.load_plan` accepts, resolving `_ProducesHandle` instances inside `repeat.for_each.from` via `str(...)`. At the end of `to_dict()`, round-trip the payload through validation: write to a temp file and call `artagents.core.task.plan.load_plan(tmp_path)` to reuse the public validator (avoids depending on the private `_validate_plan` symbol per FLAG-002), re-raising any `TaskPlanError` as `OrchestrateDefinitionError`.",

    "FLAG-002 (round-trip uses private symbol): T2 must round-trip via writing to a temp file and calling the public `artagents.core.task.plan.load_plan(path)`, NOT by importing the private `_validate_plan`. Future internal changes to plan.py would silently break the DSL otherwise.",
    "FLAG-003 (scaffold path collision): `author new builtin.hype` would write `artagents/packs/builtin/hype.py` next to the existing `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` folder-orchestrator package; on import the package shadows the module. T4 must refuse scaffolding when a same-stem folder exists, not just when a same-name file exists.",
    "FLAG-004 (`__getattr__` reserved-attr shadowing): produces names like `id`, `kind`, `produces`, `repeat`, `command`, `argv`, `plan`, `instructions`, `ack` collide with real `_StepHandle` attributes. Python only calls `__getattr__` for missing attributes, so a produces entry named `id` silently resolves to the step id string. T2 must reject such collisions at construction.",
    "FLAG-005 (nested cycle detection): inline expansion of `nested(plan=\"<pack>.<name>\")` strings recurses; a self- or mutually-recursive reference will loop forever. T3 must thread a visiting-set keyed on qualified id and raise `OrchestrateDefinitionError` on revisit.",
    "FLAG-006 (argv element type): authors will write `argv=[..., transcribe.transcript]` to embed produces refs - that is the entire point of typed handles. `shlex.join` raises on non-strings. 109	    payload = _read_plan_payload(plan_path)
110	    _validate_plan(payload)
111	    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical_event_json(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

115	def step_dir_for_path(
116	    slug: str,
117	    run_id: str,
118	    plan_step_path: tuple[str, ...],
119	    *,
120	    iteration: int | None = None,
121	    item_id: str | None = None,
122	    root: str | Path | None = None,
123	) -> Path:
124	    validate_project_slug(slug)
125	    validate_run_id(run_id)
126	    if not plan_step_path:
127	        raise TaskPlanError("plan_step_path must contain at least one segment")
128	    for segment in plan_step_path:
129	        validate_run_id(segment)
130	    if iteration is not None and item_id is not None:
131	        raise TaskPlanError("step_dir_for_path: iteration and item_id are mutually exclusive")
132	    base = project_dir(slug, root=root) / "runs" / run_id / "steps"
133	    for segment in plan_step_path:
134	        base = base / segment
135	    if iteration is not None:
136	        if not isinstance(iteration, int) or isinstance(iteration, bool) or iteration < 1:
137	            raise TaskPlanError("step_dir_for_path: iteration must be an int >= 1")
138	        base = base / "iterations" / f"{iteration:03d}"
139	    elif item_id is not None:
140	        validate_run_id(item_id)
141	        base = base / "items" / item_id
142	    return base

148	    plan_step_id: str,
149	    *,

151	) -> Path:

155	def iter_steps_with_path(plan: TaskPlan) -> Iterator[tuple[tuple[str, ...], TaskPlanStep]]:
156	    """Yield ``(path, step)`` in pre-order for every step in the plan tree."""
157	
158	    def _walk(steps: tuple[TaskPlanStep, ...], prefix: tuple[str, ...]) -> Iterator[tuple[tuple[str, ...], TaskPlanStep]]:
159	        for step in steps:
160	            path = prefix + (step.id,)
161	            yield path, step
162	            if isinstance(step, NestedStep):
163	                yield from _walk(step.plan.steps, path)
164	
165	    yield from _walk(plan.steps, ())
166

168	def parse_from_ref(from_ref: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
169	    """Parse '<step-id>.produces.<name>' into (step_id, produces_name)."""
170	    sep = ".produces."
171	    idx = from_ref.find(sep)
172	    if idx <= 0 or idx + len(sep) >= len(from_ref):
173	        raise TaskPlanError(
174	            f"repeat.for_each.from must match '<step-id>.produces.<name>', got {from_ref!r}"
175	        )
176	    return from_ref[:idx], from_ref[idx + len(sep):]
177	
178	
179	def _step_to_dict(step: TaskPlanStep) -> dict[str, Any]:
180	    if isinstance(step, CodeStep):
181	        out: dict[str, Any] = {"id": step.id, "kind": "code", "command": step.command}
182	    elif isinstance(step, AttestedStep):
183	        out = {
184	            "id": step.id,
185	            "kind": "attested",
186	            "command": step.command,
187	            "instructions": step.instructions,
188	            "ack": {"kind": step.ack.kind},
189	        }
190	    elif isinstance(step, NestedStep):
191	        out = {
192	            "id": step.id,
193	            "kind": "nested",
194	            "plan": step.plan.to_dict(),
195	        }
196	    else:
197	        raise TaskPlanError(f"unknown step type: {type(step)!r}")
198	    if step.produces:
199	        out["produces"] = _produces_to_dict(step.produces)
200	    if step.repeat is not None:
201	        out["repeat"] = _repeat_to_dict(step.repeat)
202	    return out
203	
204	
205	def _produces_to_dict(produces: tuple[ProducesEntry, ...]) -> dict[str, Any]:
206	    sorted_entries = sorted(produces, key=lambda entry: entry.name)
207	    return {
208	        entry.name: {
209	            "path": entry.path,
210	            "check": {
211	                "check_id": entry.check.check_id,
212	                "params": canonical_check_params(entry.check.params),
213	                "sentinel": entry.check.sentinel,
214	            },
215	        }
216	        for entry in sorted_entries
217	    }
218	
219	
220	def _repeat_to_dict(repeat: Repeat) -> dict[str, Any]:
221	    if isinstance(repeat, RepeatUntil):
222	        out: dict[str, Any] = {
223	            "until": repeat.condition,
224	            "max_iterations": repeat.max_iterations,
225	            "on_exhaust": repeat.on_exhaust,
226	        }
227	        if repeat.quorum_n is not None:
228	            out["quorum_n"] = repeat.quorum_n
229	        return out
230	    if isinstance(repeat, RepeatForEach):
231	        if repeat.items_source == "static":
232	            return {"for_each": {"items": list(repeat.items)}}
233	        return {"for_each": {"from": repeat.from_ref}}
234	    raise TaskPlanError(f"unknown repeat type: {type(repeat)!r}")

237	def _read_plan_payload(plan_path: str | Path) -> Any:
238	    path = Path(plan_path)
239	    try:
240	        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
241	    except FileNotFoundError:
242	        raise
243	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
244	        raise TaskPlanError(f"invalid JSON in {path}: {exc.msg}") from exc
245	    except OSError as exc:
246	        raise TaskPlanError(f"failed to read {path}: {exc}") from exc
247

249	def _validate_plan(payload: Any, *, _is_root: bool = True) -> TaskPlan:
250	    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
251	        raise TaskPlanError("plan must be an object")
252	    plan_id = payload.get("plan_id")
253	    version = payload.get("version")
254	    steps = payload.get("steps")
255	    if not isinstance(plan_id, str) or not plan_id:
256	        raise TaskPlanError("plan plan_id must be a non-empty string")
257	    if version != 1 or isinstance(version, bool):
258	        raise TaskPlanError("plan version must be 1")
259	    if not isinstance(steps, list):
260	        raise TaskPlanError("plan steps must be a list")
261	
262	    validated_steps: list[TaskPlanStep] = []
263	    for index, step in enumerate(steps):
264	        validated_steps.append(_validate_step(step, index, validated_steps))
265	
266	    plan = TaskPlan(plan_id=plan_id, version=1, steps=tuple(validated_steps))
267	    if _is_root:
268	        _assert_unique_paths(plan)
269	    return plan
270	
271	
272	def _validate_step(step: Any, index: int, prior_siblings: list[TaskPlanStep]) -> TaskPlanStep:
273	    if not isinstance(step, dict):
274	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}] must be an object")
275	    kind = step.get("kind", "code")
276	    if kind == "code":
277	        return _validate_code_step(step, index, prior_siblings)
278	    if kind == "attested":
279	        return _validate_attested_step(step, index, prior_siblings)
280	    if kind == "nested":
281	        return _validate_nested_step(step, index, prior_siblings)
282	    raise TaskPlanError(
283	        f"plan steps[{index}].kind must be one of {TASK_STEP_KINDS}, got {kind!r}"
284	    )

287	def _validate_code_step(step: dict[str, Any], index: int, prior_siblings: list[TaskPlanStep]) -> CodeStep:
288	    step_id = step.get("id")
289	    command = step.get("command")
290	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id:
291	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].id must be a non-empty string")
292	    if not isinstance(command, str) or not command:
293	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].command must be a non-empty string")
294	    _reject_orchestrators_run(command, index)
295	    produces = _validate_produces(step.get("produces"), index, allow_legacy_list=True)
296	    repeat = _validate_repeat(step.get("repeat"), index, prior_siblings)

305	def _validate_attested_step(step: dict[str, Any], index: int, prior_siblings: list[TaskPlanStep]) -> AttestedStep:
306	    step_id = step.get("id")
307	    command = step.get("command")
308	    instructions = step.get("instructions")
309	    raw_produces = step.get("produces")
310	    ack = step.get("ack")
311	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id:
312	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].id must be a non-empty string")
313	    if not isinstance(command, str) or not command:
314	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].command must be a non-empty string")
315	    if not isinstance(instructions, str) or not instructions:
316	        raise TaskPlanError(
317	            f"plan steps[{index}].instructions must be a non-empty string for attested step"
318	        )
319	    if isinstance(raw_produces, list) and raw_produces:
320	        raise TaskPlanError(
321	            f"plan steps[{index}].produces is a sentinel-only list; attested produces require a semantic check (use dict form with non-sentinel check)"
322	        )
323	    produces = _validate_produces(raw_produces, index, allow_legacy_list=False)
324	    for entry in produces:
325	        if entry.check.sentinel:
326	            raise TaskPlanError(
327	                f"plan steps[{index}].produces[{entry.name!r}] uses sentinel-only check {entry.check.check_id!r}; attested produces requires a semantic check"
328	            )
329	    if not isinstance(ack, dict):
330	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].ack must be an object")
331	    ack_kind = ack.get("kind")
332	    if ack_kind not in {"agent", "actor"}:
333	        raise TaskPlanError(
334	            f"plan steps[{index}].ack.kind must be 'agent' or 'actor', got {ack_kind!r}"
335	        )
336	    repeat = _validate_repeat(step.get("repeat"), index, prior_siblings)
337	    return AttestedStep(
338	        id=validate_run_id(step_id),
339	        command=command,
340	        instructions=instructions,
341	        produces=produces,
342	        ack=AckRule(kind=ack_kind),
343	        repeat=repeat,
344	    )

347	def _validate_nested_step(step: dict[str, Any], index: int, prior_siblings: list[TaskPlanStep]) -> NestedStep:
348	    step_id = step.get("id")
349	    child_plan = step.get("plan")
350	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id:
351	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].id must be a non-empty string")
352	    if not isinstance(child_plan, dict):
353	        raise TaskPlanError(
354	            f"plan steps[{index}].plan must be a nested plan object"
355	        )
356	    nested = _validate_plan(child_plan, _is_root=False)
357	    produces = _validate_produces(step.get("produces"), index, allow_legacy_list=False)
358	    repeat = _validate_repeat(step.get("repeat"), index, prior_siblings)
359	    return NestedStep(
360	        id=validate_run_id(step_id),
361	        plan=nested,
362	        produces=produces,
363	        repeat=repeat,
364	    )

366	
367	def _validate_produces(raw: Any, index: int, *, allow_legacy_list: bool) -> tuple[ProducesEntry, ...]:
368	    if raw is None:
369	        return ()
370	    if isinstance(raw, list):
371	        if not allow_legacy_list:
372	            raise TaskPlanError(
373	                f"plan steps[{index}].produces must be a dict; legacy list form not allowed for this step kind"
374	            )
375	        if not raw:
376	            return ()
377	        entries: list[ProducesEntry] = []
378	        seen: set[str] = set()
379	        for item in raw:
380	            if not isinstance(item, str) or not item:
381	                raise TaskPlanError(
382	                    f"plan steps[{index}].produces legacy list must contain non-empty strings"
383	                )
384	            name = Path(item).stem or item
385	            if name in seen:
386	                raise TaskPlanError(
387	                    f"plan steps[{index}].produces legacy list yields duplicate name {name!r}; use dict form"
388	                )
389	            seen.add(name)
390	            entries.append(ProducesEntry(name=name, path=item, check=file_nonempty()))
391	        return tuple(entries)
392	    if isinstance(raw, dict):
393	        entries = []
394	        for name in raw:
395	            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name:
396	                raise TaskPlanError(
397	                    f"plan steps[{index}].produces keys must be non-empty strings"
398	                )
399	            value = raw[name]
400	            if not isinstance(value, dict):
401	                raise TaskPlanError(
402	                    f"plan steps[{index}].produces[{name!r}] must be an object"
403	                )
404	            path_value = value.get("path")
405	            if not isinstance(path_value, str) or not path_value:
406	                raise TaskPlanError(
407	                    f"plan steps[{index}].produces[{name!r}].path must be a non-empty string"
408	                )
409	            check = _validate_check(value.get("check"), index, name)
410	            entries.append(ProducesEntry(name=name, path=path_value, check=check))
411	        return tuple(entries)
412	    raise TaskPlanError(
413	        f"plan steps[{index}].produces must be a dict (or legacy list for code steps)"
414	    )

417	def _validate_check(raw: Any, index: int, produces_name: str) -> Check:
418	    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
419	        raise TaskPlanError(
420	            f"plan steps[{index}].produces[{produces_name!r}].check must be an object"
421	        )
422	    check_id = raw.get("check_id")
423	    if not isinstance(check_id, str) or not check_id:
424	        raise TaskPlanError(
425	            f"plan steps[{index}].produces[{produces_name!r}].check.check_id must be a non-empty string"
426	        )
427	    params = raw.get("params", {})
428	    if not isinstance(params, dict):
429	        raise TaskPlanError(

431	        )
432	    sentinel = raw.get("sentinel", False)
433	    if not isinstance(sentinel, bool):
434	        raise TaskPlanError(

436	        )
437	    return Check(check_id=check_id, params=canonical_check_params(params), sentinel=sentinel)

439	
440	def _validate_repeat(raw: Any, index: int, prior_siblings: list[TaskPlanStep]) -> Repeat | None:
441	    if raw is None:
442	        return None
443	    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
444	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].repeat must be an object")
445	    has_until = "until" in raw
446	    has_for_each = "for_each" in raw
447	    if has_until and has_for_each:
448	        raise TaskPlanError(
449	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat cannot have both 'until' and 'for_each'"
450	        )
451	    if not has_until and not has_for_each:
452	        raise TaskPlanError(
453	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat must contain 'until' or 'for_each'"
454	        )
455	    if has_until:
456	        return _validate_repeat_until(raw, index)
457	    return _validate_repeat_for_each(raw["for_each"], index, prior_siblings)
458	
459	
460	def _validate_repeat_until(raw: dict[str, Any], index: int) -> RepeatUntil:
461	    condition = raw.get("until")
462	    if condition not in {"user_approves", "verifier_passes", "quorum"}:
463	        raise TaskPlanError(
464	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.until must be one of 'user_approves','verifier_passes','quorum', got {condition!r}"
465	        )
466	    max_iterations = raw.get("max_iterations")
467	    if not isinstance(max_iterations, int) or isinstance(max_iterations, bool) or max_iterations < 1:
468	        raise TaskPlanError(
469	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.max_iterations must be an int >= 1"
470	        )
471	    on_exhaust = raw.get("on_exhaust")
472	    if on_exhaust not in {"escalate", "fail"}:
473	        raise TaskPlanError(
474	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.on_exhaust must be 'escalate' or 'fail', got {on_exhaust!r}"
475	        )
476	    quorum_n = raw.get("quorum_n")
477	    if condition == "quorum":
478	        if not isinstance(quorum_n, int) or isinstance(quorum_n, bool) or quorum_n < 1:
479	            raise TaskPlanError(
480	                f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.quorum_n must be an int >= 1 when until='quorum'"
481	            )
482	    elif quorum_n is not None:
483	        raise TaskPlanError(
484	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.quorum_n only valid when until='quorum'"
485	        )
486	    return RepeatUntil(
487	        condition=condition,
488	        max_iterations=max_iterations,
489	        on_exhaust=on_exhaust,
490	        quorum_n=quorum_n,
491	    )

494	def _validate_repeat_for_each(raw: Any, index: int, prior_siblings: list[TaskPlanStep]) -> RepeatForEach:
495	    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
496	        raise TaskPlanError(
497	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each must be an object"
498	        )
499	    has_items = "items" in raw
500	    has_from = "from" in raw
501	    if has_items == has_from:
502	        raise TaskPlanError(
503	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each must have exactly one of 'items' or 'from'"
504	        )
505	    if has_items:
506	        items = raw["items"]
507	        if not isinstance(items, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) and x for x in items):
508	            raise TaskPlanError(
509	                f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each.items must be a list of non-empty strings"
510	            )
511	        return RepeatForEach(items_source="static", items=tuple(items), from_ref=None)
512	    from_ref = raw["from"]
513	    if not isinstance(from_ref, str) or not from_ref:
514	        raise TaskPlanError(
515	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each.from must be a non-empty string"
516	        )
517	    target_id, produces_name = parse_from_ref(from_ref)
518	    target = next((s for s in prior_siblings if s.id == target_id), None)
519	    if target is None:
520	        raise TaskPlanError(
521	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each.from references unknown prior sibling step {target_id!r}"
522	        )
523	    if not any(p.name == produces_name for p in target.produces):
524	        raise TaskPlanError(
525	            f"plan steps[{index}].repeat.for_each.from references unknown produces {produces_name!r} on step {target_id!r}"
526	        )
527	    return RepeatForEach(items_source="from", items=(), from_ref=from_ref)
528	
529	
530	def _reject_orchestrators_run(command: str, index: int) -> None:
531	    try:
532	        tokens = shlex.split(command)
533	    except ValueError as exc:
534	        raise TaskPlanError(
535	            f"plan steps[{index}].command is not shell-parseable: {exc}"
536	        ) from exc
537	    tokens = _strip_artagents_prefix(tokens)
538	    if len(tokens) >= 2 and tokens[0] == "orchestrators" and tokens[1] == "run":
539	        raise TaskPlanError(
540	            "code step argv targets 'artagents orchestrators run'; use a nested step"
541	        )
542	
543	
544	def _strip_artagents_prefix(tokens: list[str]) -> list[str]:
545	    if (
546	        len(tokens) >= 3
547	        and Path(tokens[0]).name.startswith("python")
548	        and tokens[1:3] == ["-m", "artagents"]
549	    ):
550	        return tokens[3:]
551	    if tokens and Path(tokens[0]).name.endswith("artagents"):
552	        return tokens[1:]
553	    return tokens
554	
555	
556	def _assert_unique_paths(plan: TaskPlan) -> None:
557	    """Reject sibling-id collisions inside any one frame.
558	
559	    Identical leaf ids in different subtrees are accepted because their full paths differ.
560	    """
561	
562	    def _check(steps: tuple[TaskPlanStep, ...], prefix: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
563	        seen: set[str] = set()
564	        for step in steps:
565	            if step.id in seen:
566	                location = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(prefix + (step.id,))
567	                raise TaskPlanError(
568	                    f"duplicate step id {step.id!r} among siblings at {location!r}"
569	                )
570	            seen.add(step.id)
571	            if isinstance(step, NestedStep):
572	                _check(step.plan.steps, prefix + (step.id,))
573	
574	    _check(plan.steps, ())
575

1	"""Inline produces-check primitives.
2	
3	Each factory returns a frozen ``Check`` whose ``run(path)`` produces a
4	``CheckResult``. Checks are stdlib-only — ``wave`` for WAV duration,
5	``ffprobe`` subprocess fallback for non-WAV audio, raw PNG/JPEG header
6	parsing for image_dimensions.
7	
8	``sentinel`` flags whether a check is sentinel-only (existence-only).
9	``file_nonempty`` is sentinel-only; the rest are semantic. ``all_of`` is
10	sentinel iff every constituent is sentinel.
11	"""
12	
13	from __future__ import annotations
14	
15	import json
16	import subprocess
17	import wave
18	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
19	from pathlib import Path
20	from typing import Any, Callable
21	
22	
23	@dataclass(frozen=True)
24	class CheckResult:
25	    ok: bool
26	    reason: str = ""
27	    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
28	
29	
30	_RUNNER_REGISTRY: dict[str, Callable[[Path, dict[str, Any]], CheckResult]] = {}
31	
32	
33	@dataclass(frozen=True)
34	class Check:
35	    check_id: str
36	    params: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
37	    sentinel: bool = False
38	
39	    def run(self, path: Path) -> CheckResult:
40	        runner = _RUNNER_REGISTRY.get(self.check_id)
41	        if runner is None:
42	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"unknown check_id: {self.check_id}")
43	        return runner(path, self.params)
44	
45	
46	def canonical_check_params(params: Any) -> Any:
47	    if isinstance(params, dict):
48	        return {k: canonical_check_params(params[k]) for k in sorted(params)}
49	    if isinstance(params, (list, tuple)):
50	        return [canonical_check_params(item) for item in params]
51	    return params
52	
53	
54	def _register(check_id: str) -> Callable[[Callable[[Path, dict[str, Any]], CheckResult]], Callable[[Path, dict[str, Any]], CheckResult]]:
55	    def decorator(fn: Callable[[Path, dict[str, Any]], CheckResult]) -> Callable[[Path, dict[str, Any]], CheckResult]:
56	        _RUNNER_REGISTRY[check_id] = fn
57	        return fn
58	    return decorator
59	
60	
61	def file_nonempty() -> Check:
62	    return Check(check_id="file_nonempty", params={}, sentinel=True)
63	
64	
65	@_register("file_nonempty")
66	def _run_file_nonempty(path: Path, _params: dict[str, Any]) -> CheckResult:
67	    if not path.exists():
68	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"file does not exist: {path}")
69	    try:
70	        size = path.stat().st_size
71	    except OSError as exc:
72	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"stat failed: {exc}")
73	    if size <= 0:
74	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"file is empty: {path}", details={"size": size})
75	    return CheckResult(ok=True, details={"size": size})
76	
77	
78	def json_file() -> Check:
79	    return Check(check_id="json_file", params={}, sentinel=False)
80	
81	
82	@_register("json_file")
83	def _run_json_file(path: Path, _params: dict[str, Any]) -> CheckResult:
84	    try:
85	        raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
86	    except FileNotFoundError:
87	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"file does not exist: {path}")
88	    except OSError as exc:
89	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"read failed: {exc}")
90	    try:
91	        json.loads(raw)
92	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
93	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"invalid JSON: {exc.msg}")
94	    return CheckResult(ok=True)
95	
96	
97	def json_schema(schema: dict[str, Any]) -> Check:
98	    return Check(check_id="json_schema", params={"schema": canonical_check_params(schema)}, sentinel=False)
99	
100	
101	@_register("json_schema")
102	def _run_json_schema(path: Path, params: dict[str, Any]) -> CheckResult:
103	    try:
104	        payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
105	    except FileNotFoundError:
106	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"file does not exist: {path}")
107	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
108	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"invalid JSON: {exc.msg}")
109	    except OSError as exc:
110	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"read failed: {exc}")
111	    schema = params.get("schema") or {}
112	    required = schema.get("required") or []
113	    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
114	        if required:
115	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason="payload is not an object")
116	        return CheckResult(ok=True)
117	    for key in required:
118	        if key not in payload:
119	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"missing required key: {key}")
120	    return CheckResult(ok=True)
121	
122	
123	def audio_duration_min(seconds: float) -> Check:
124	    return Check(check_id="audio_duration_min", params={"seconds": float(seconds)}, sentinel=False)
125	
126	
127	@_register("audio_duration_min")
128	def _run_audio_duration_min(path: Path, params: dict[str, Any]) -> CheckResult:
129	    minimum = float(params.get("seconds", 0.0))
130	    if not path.exists():
131	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"file does not exist: {path}")
132	    duration: float | None = None
133	    if path.suffix.lower() == ".wav":
134	        try:
135	            with wave.open(str(path), "rb") as handle:
136	                frames = handle.getnframes()
137	                rate = handle.getframerate() or 1
138	                duration = frames / float(rate)
139	        except (wave.Error, EOFError) as exc:
140	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"wave parse failed: {exc}")
141	    else:
142	        try:
143	            result = subprocess.run(
144	                ["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration",
145	                 "-of", "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1", str(path)],
146	                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, check=True,
147	            )
148	        except FileNotFoundError:
149	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason="ffprobe not on PATH")
150	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
151	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason="ffprobe timed out")
152	        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
153	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"ffprobe failed: {exc.stderr.strip() if exc.stderr else exc}")
154	        try:
155	            duration = float(result.stdout.strip())
156	        except ValueError:
157	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"ffprobe returned non-numeric duration: {result.stdout!r}")
158	    if duration is None or duration < minimum:
159	        return CheckResult(
160	            ok=False,
161	            reason=f"duration {duration!r} below minimum {minimum}",
162	            details={"duration": duration, "minimum": minimum},
163	        )
164	    return CheckResult(ok=True, details={"duration": duration, "minimum": minimum})
165	
166	
167	def image_dimensions(*, min_w: int = 0, min_h: int = 0) -> Check:
168	    return Check(
169	        check_id="image_dimensions",
170	        params={"min_w": int(min_w), "min_h": int(min_h)},
171	        sentinel=False,
172	    )
173	
174	
175	@_register("image_dimensions")
176	def _run_image_dimensions(path: Path, params: dict[str, Any]) -> CheckResult:
177	    min_w = int(params.get("min_w", 0))
178	    min_h = int(params.get("min_h", 0))
179	    try:
180	        with path.open("rb") as handle:
181	            head = handle.read(32)
182	    except FileNotFoundError:
183	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"file does not exist: {path}")
184	    except OSError as exc:
185	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"read failed: {exc}")
186	    width: int | None = None
187	    height: int | None = None
188	    if head.startswith(b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n") and len(head) >= 24:
189	        width = int.from_bytes(head[16:20], "big")
190	        height = int.from_bytes(head[20:24], "big")
191	    elif head.startswith(b"\xff\xd8"):
192	        width, height = _parse_jpeg_dimensions(path)
193	    if width is None or height is None:
194	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason="could not parse image dimensions")
195	    if width < min_w or height < min_h:
196	        return CheckResult(
197	            ok=False,
198	            reason=f"image {width}x{height} below minimum {min_w}x{min_h}",
199	            details={"width": width, "height": height, "min_w": min_w, "min_h": min_h},
200	        )
201	    return CheckResult(ok=True, details={"width": width, "height": height})
202	
203	
204	def _parse_jpeg_dimensions(path: Path) -> tuple[int | None, int | None]:
205	    try:
206	        with path.open("rb") as handle:
207	            handle.read(2)
208	            while True:
209	                marker = handle.read(2)
210	                if len(marker) < 2 or marker[0] != 0xFF:
211	                    return None, None
212	                code = marker[1]
213	                if code in (0xD8, 0xD9):
214	                    return None, None
215	                size_bytes = handle.read(2)
216	                if len(size_bytes) < 2:
217	                    return None, None
218	                seg_len = int.from_bytes(size_bytes, "big")
219	                if code in (0xC0, 0xC1, 0xC2, 0xC3, 0xC5, 0xC6, 0xC7, 0xC9, 0xCA, 0xCB, 0xCD, 0xCE, 0xCF):
220	                    payload = handle.read(seg_len - 2)
221	                    if len(payload) < 5:
222	                        return None, None
223	                    height = int.from_bytes(payload[1:3], "big")
224	                    width = int.from_bytes(payload[3:5], "big")
225	                    return width, height
226	                handle.seek(seg_len - 2, 1)
227	    except OSError:
228	        return None, None
229	
230	
231	def all_of(*checks: Check) -> Check:
232	    if not checks:
233	        raise ValueError("all_of requires at least one check")
234	    sentinel = all(c.sentinel for c in checks)
235	    return Check(
236	        check_id="all_of",
237	        params={"checks": [{"check_id": c.check_id, "params": canonical_check_params(c.params), "sentinel": c.sentinel} for c in checks]},
238	        sentinel=sentinel,
239	    )
240	
241	
242	@_register("all_of")
243	def _run_all_of(path: Path, params: dict[str, Any]) -> CheckResult:
244	    items = params.get("checks") or []
245	    for entry in items:
246	        check_id = entry.get("check_id")
247	        runner = _RUNNER_REGISTRY.get(check_id)
248	        if runner is None:
249	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"unknown check_id: {check_id}")
250	        result = runner(path, entry.get("params") or {})
251	        if not result.ok:
252	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"all_of failed at {check_id}: {result.reason}", details=result.details)
253	    return CheckResult(ok=True)

1	"""Repository structure guardrails for ArtAgents canonical concepts."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	from dataclasses import dataclass

9	from artagents.core.executor.folder import load_folder_executors

11	
12	
13	LEGACY_PUBLIC_DIRS = ("conductors", "performers", "instruments", "primitives", "executors", "orchestrators")
14	LEGACY_LOCAL_DIRS = ("performers", "conductors", "nodes", "instruments", "primitives")
15	INTERNAL_PACK_DIRS = {"__pycache__"}
16	TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_FILES = {
17	    "__init__.py",
18	    "__main__.py",
19	    "_paths.py",
20	    "doctor.py",
21	    "pipeline.py",
22	    "setup_cli.py",
23	    "structure.py",
24	    "theme_schema.py",
25	    "timeline.py",
26	}
27	TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS = {
28	    "__pycache__",
29	    "audit",
30	    "contracts",
31	    "core",
32	    "domains",
33	    "elements",
34	    "modalities",
35	    "packs",

41	
42	@dataclass(frozen=True)
43	class StructureReport:
44	    errors: tuple[str, ...]
45	
46	    @property
47	    def ok(self) -> bool:
48	        return not self.errors
49	
50	
51	def validate_repo_structure(root: str | Path = REPO_ROOT) -> StructureReport:
52	    repo_root = Path(root)
53	    errors: list[str] = []
54	    errors.extend(_validate_legacy_dirs(repo_root))
55	    errors.extend(_validate_local_state_dirs(repo_root))
56	    errors.extend(_validate_top_level_artagents(repo_root / "artagents"))
57	    errors.extend(_validate_pack_executor_folders(repo_root / "artagents" / "packs"))
58	    errors.extend(_validate_pack_orchestrator_folders(repo_root / "artagents" / "packs"))
59	    errors.extend(_validate_pack_element_folders(repo_root / "artagents" / "packs"))
60	    return StructureReport(errors=tuple(errors))
61	
62	
63	def _validate_legacy_dirs(repo_root: Path) -> list[str]:
64	    errors: list[str] = []
65	    for dirname in LEGACY_PUBLIC_DIRS:
66	        candidate = repo_root / "artagents" / dirname
67	        if candidate.exists():
68	            errors.append(f"legacy public package must not exist: {candidate.relative_to(repo_root)}")
69	    return errors
70	
71	
72	def _validate_local_state_dirs(repo_root: Path) -> list[str]:
73	    errors: list[str] = []
74	    local_root = repo_root / ".artagents"
75	    if not local_root.exists():
76	        return errors
77	    for dirname in LEGACY_LOCAL_DIRS:
78	        candidate = local_root / dirname
79	        if candidate.exists():
80	            errors.append(f"legacy local state directory must not exist: {candidate.relative_to(repo_root)}")
81	    return errors
82	
83	
84	def _validate_top_level_artagents(package_root: Path) -> list[str]:
85	    errors: list[str] = []
86	    for child in sorted(package_root.iterdir()):
87	        if child.name.startswith("."):
88	            continue
89	        if child.is_file() and child.suffix == ".py" and child.name not in TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_FILES:
90	            errors.append(f"top-level artagents module must move to a canonical package: {child.relative_to(package_root.parents[0])}")
91	        if child.is_dir() and child.name not in TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS:
92	            errors.append(f"top-level artagents directory is not a canonical concept: {child.relative_to(package_root.parents[0])}")
93	    return errors
94	
95	
96	def _validate_pack_executor_folders(packs_root: Path) -> list[str]:
97	    if not packs_root.is_dir():
98	        return [f"missing packs directory: {packs_root}"]
99	
100	    errors: list[str] = []
101	    repo_root = packs_root.parents[1]
102	    for pack_dir in _public_child_dirs(packs_root, INTERNAL_PACK_DIRS):
103	        for folder in _public_child_dirs(pack_dir, INTERNAL_PACK_DIRS):
104	            if not _has_any(folder, ("executor.yaml", "executor.yml", "executor.json", "executor.py")):
105	                continue
106	            errors.extend(_require_files(folder, ("executor.yaml", "run.py", "STAGE.md"), root=repo_root))
107	            if _has_any(folder, ("orchestrator.yaml", "orchestrator.yml", "orchestrator.json", "orchestrator.py")):
108	                errors.append(f"executor folder contains orchestrator metadata: {folder.relative_to(repo_root)}")
109	            try:
110	                definitions = load_folder_executors(folder)
111	            except Exception as exc:
112	                errors.append(f"invalid executor folder {folder.relative_to(repo_root)}: {exc}")
113	                continue
114	            if not definitions:
115	                errors.append(f"executor folder emitted no executor metadata: {folder.relative_to(repo_root)}")
116	                continue
117	            for definition in definitions:
118	                pack_segment = definition.id.split(".", 1)[0]
119	                if pack_segment != pack_dir.name:
120	                    errors.append(
121	                        f"executor {definition.id!r} must live in pack {pack_segment!r} but was found in pack {pack_dir.name!r}"
122	                    )
123	    return errors
124	
125	
126	def _validate_pack_orchestrator_folders(packs_root: Path) -> list[str]:
127	    if not packs_root.is_dir():
128	        return [f"missing packs directory: {packs_root}"]
129	
130	    errors: list[str] = []
131	    repo_root = packs_root.parents[1]
132	    for pack_dir in _public_child_dirs(packs_root, INTERNAL_PACK_DIRS):
133	        for folder in _public_child_dirs(pack_dir, INTERNAL_PACK_DIRS):
134	            if not _has_any(folder, ("orchestrator.yaml", "orchestrator.yml", "orchestrator.json", "orchestrator.py")):
135	                continue
136	            errors.extend(_require_files(folder, ("orchestrator.yaml", "run.py", "STAGE.md"), root=repo_root))
137	            if _has_any(folder, ("executor.yaml", "executor.yml", "executor.json", "executor.py")):
138	                errors.append(f"orchestrator folder contains executor metadata: {folder.relative_to(repo_root)}")
139	            try:
140	                definitions = load_folder_orchestrators(folder)
141	            except Exception as exc:
142	                errors.append(f"invalid orchestrator folder {folder.relative_to(repo_root)}: {exc}")
143	                continue
144	            if not definitions:
145	                errors.append(f"orchestrator folder emitted no orchestrator metadata: {folder.relative_to(repo_root)}")
146	                continue
147	            for definition in definitions:
148	                pack_segment = definition.id.split(".", 1)[0]
149	                if pack_segment != pack_dir.name:
150	                    errors.append(
151	                        f"orchestrator {definition.id!r} must live in pack {pack_segment!r} but was found in pack {pack_dir.name!r}"
152	                    )
153	    return errors
154	
155	
156	_ELEMENT_KINDS = ("effects", "animations", "transitions")
157	
158	
159	def _validate_pack_element_folders(packs_root: Path) -> list[str]:
160	    if not packs_root.is_dir():
161	        return []
162	
163	    errors: list[str] = []
164	    repo_root = packs_root.parents[1]
165	    for pack_dir in _public_child_dirs(packs_root, INTERNAL_PACK_DIRS):
166	        elements_root = pack_dir / "elements"
167	        if not elements_root.is_dir():
168	            continue
169	        for kind_dir in _public_child_dirs(elements_root, INTERNAL_PACK_DIRS):
170	            if kind_dir.name not in _ELEMENT_KINDS:
171	                errors.append(
172	                    f"unexpected element kind folder {kind_dir.relative_to(repo_root)}: must be one of {list(_ELEMENT_KINDS)}"
173	                )
174	                continue
175	            for element_dir in _public_child_dirs(kind_dir, INTERNAL_PACK_DIRS):
176	                errors.extend(_require_files(element_dir, ("component.tsx", "element.yaml"), root=repo_root))
177	    return errors
178	
179	
180	def _public_child_dirs(root: Path, skipped: set[str]) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
181	    return tuple(sorted(path for path in root.iterdir() if path.is_dir() and path.name not in skipped and not path.name.startswith(".")))
182	
183	
184	def _require_files(folder: Path, filenames: tuple[str, ...], *, root: Path) -> list[str]:
185	    return [f"{folder.relative_to(root)} missing {filename}" for filename in filenames if not (folder / filename).is_file()]
186	
187	
188	def _has_any(folder: Path, filenames: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
189	    return any((folder / filename).exists() for filename in filenames)
190	
191	
192	__all__ = ["StructureReport", "validate_repo_structure"]
193

29:runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/
42:`attested` is agent or human work with instructions, produces, identity-pinned ack, and evidence. Agent attestations require `--agent <id>`. Human attestations require `--actor <name>` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected.
52:`produces` declares artifacts and checks beside the step that creates or attests them, using `artagents.verify` helpers: `file_nonempty`, `json_schema`, `json_file`, `audio_duration_min`, `image_dimensions`, and `all_of`. The gate runs checks after completion or ack; failure records the verifier output and rewinds the cursor. `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step. Sentinel-existence-only checks are rejected for non-trivial attested outputs.
74:`author check` is sub-second static validation: schema, references, nested plan resolution, semantic checks for non-trivial attested produces, sentinel-only rejection, and rejection of `code` argv to `artagents orchestrators run`. `author describe` prints the DAG. `author test --fixture` runs `--dry-run --auto-approve` and diffs `events.jsonl` against the fixture golden.
100:- Scope: inline produces checks, cursor rewind, `repeat.until`, and `repeat.for_each`.
132:- Scope: `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` and symlink-based produces.
135:- Exit criteria: artifacts store once and link into step produces; no shared CAS exists.

165:    code("render", argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.render", "..."], produces={"video": file_nonempty("hype.mp4")}),

193:Phase 3 ships a useful frozen-plan runner: gate, three step kinds, produces checks, and repeat/fan-out. Phase 5 ships the author-friendly CLI surface. Phases 6-9 are polish and scale: nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden author tests.
206:- A10 Confirmed: task mode skips child `run.json` records but preserves child output dirs and hype artifact mirroring under parent step produces.
245:SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
251:SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
275:Three `prepare_project_run` callers (orchestrator runner, executor runner, hype direct entry) honor the env contract via the centralized branch inside `prepare_project_run` keyed on `is_in_task_run(slug)`; `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` / `ARTAGENTS_TASK_PROJECT` / `ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID` redirect run output under `<projects_root>/<slug>/runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/` and suppress child `run.json` / cumulative `runs.json` writes; hype artifacts mirror under `<step_dir>/produces/` (SD-018). `ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID` is a Phase 1-introduced sibling env var the design doc names alongside `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID`. `command_for_argv(argv)` produces the canonical command-string form that authored `plan.json` step commands must match exactly, including the `--project <slug>` token.
279:Deferred: full subprocess env propagation into child code-step launchers (Phase 2; Phase 1 only ships the `child_subprocess_env()` helper used by the e2e smoke); lifecycle verbs `artagents next` / `abort` / `approve` / `retry` / `iterate` (Phase 5); per-project CAS at `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` (Phase 7, SD-008). New step kinds (`code`, `attested`, `nested`), produces inline checks, and the Python authoring DSL all remain out of Phase 1 scope.

  "content": "\"\"\"DSL builders for ArtAgents task-mode orchestrators (Phase 4).\n\nAuthors construct task plans in Python using the helpers exported from\n``artagents.orchestrate``. on\n``step.<missing_produces>``, sentinel-only attested rejection, the\n``orchestrators run`` argv guard, reserved-attribute collisions, and\nduplicate sibling-id detection all raise ``OrchestrateDefinitionError`` at\ndefinition time so authoring errors surface immediately.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport shlex\nimport tempfile\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Any, Callable, Optional, Tuple, Union\n\nfrom artagents.core.task.plan import (\n    TaskPlanError,\n    _strip_artagents_prefix,\n    load_plan,\n)\nfrom artagents.verify import Check, canonical_check_params\n\n\nclass OrchestrateDefinitionError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when an orchestrator DSL definition is invalid.\"\"\"\n\n\n_RESERVED_ATTRS = frozenset(\n    {\"id\", \"kind\", \"command\", \"argv\", \"plan\", \"produces\", \"repeat\", \"instructions\", \"ack\"}\n)\n_VALID_UNTIL = frozenset({\"user_approves\", \"verifier_passes\", \"quorum\"})\n_VALID_ON_EXHAUST = frozenset({\"escalate\", \"fail\"})\n_VALID_ACK_KINDS = frozenset({\"agent\", \"actor\"})\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass _ProducesHandle:\n    step_id: str\n    name: str\n\n    def __str__(self) -> str:\n        return f\"{self.step_id}.produces.{self.name}\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass _RepeatUntilSpec:\n    condition: str\n    max_iterations: int\n    on_exhaust: str\n    quorum_n: Optional[int] = None\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass _RepeatForEachSpec:\n    items: Optional[Tuple[str, ...]]\n    from_ref: Any  # str | _ProducesHandle | None\n\n\n_RepeatSpec = Union[_RepeatUntilSpec, _RepeatForEachSpec]\n\n\nclass _StepHandle:\n    \"\"\"A typed reference to a declared step.\n\n    Attribute access on an unknown name first looks up declared produces and\n    returns a ``_ProducesHandle``; misses raise ``OrchestrateDefinitionError``\n    so authoring typos fail at definition time rather than at runtime.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        id: str,\n        kind: str,\n        command: Optional[str] = None,\n        argv: Optional[Tuple[str, ...]] = None,\n        plan: Any = None,\n        produces: Optional[dict] = None,\n        repeat: Optional[_RepeatSpec] = None,\n        instructions: Optional[str] = None,\n        ack: Optional[dict] = None,\n        cost_hint_usd: Optional[float] = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self.id = id\n        self.kind = kind\n        self.command = command\n        self.argv = argv\n        self.plan = plan\n        self.produces = produces or {}\n        self.repeat = repeat\n        self.instructions = instructions\n        self.ack = ack\n        self.cost_hint_usd = cost_hint_usd\n\n    def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> _ProducesHandle:\n        produces = self.__dict__.get(\"produces\", {})\n        if name in produces:\n            return _ProducesHandle(self.__dict__.get(\"id\", \"<unknown>\"), name)\n        step_id = self.__dict__.get(\"id\", \"<unknown>\")\n        declared = \", \".join(sorted(produces.keys())) if produces else \"<none>\"\n        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n            f\"step {step_id!r} has no produces {name!r}; declared produces: [{declared}]\"\n        )\n\n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\n        return f\"_StepHandle(id={self.id!r}, kind={self.kind!r})\"\n\n\nclass _PlanBuilder:\n    \"\"\"A tree of steps; emits a load_plan-compatible JSON payload.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, plan_id: str, steps: list) -> None:\n        if not isinstance(plan_id, str) or not plan_id:\n            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\"plan_id must be a non-empty string\")\n        if not isinstance(steps, list):\n            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n                f\"plan {plan_id!r} steps must be a list, got {type(steps).__name__}\"\n            )\n        seen: set = set()\n        for index, step in enumerate(steps):\n            if not isinstance(step, _StepHandle):\n                raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n                    f\"plan {plan_id!r} steps[{index}] must be a _StepHandle, \"\n                    f\"got {type(step).__name__}\"\n                )\n            if step.id in seen:\n                raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n                    f\"plan {plan_id!r} has duplicate sibling step id {step.id!r}\"\n                )\n         Idea:
Implement Phase 4 (Python DSL + verify helpers + author check/describe/new) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md sections 10 and 14. Phase 4 scope: ship `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL as the only committed authoring representation. Authors write `<pack>/<orch>.py` using decorators/helpers like `@orchestrator`, `code()`, `attested()`, `nested()`. Step references are typed (e.g., `step.notes` resolves a produces handle, typo-checked at definition). The DSL emits a validated definition equivalent to the JSON manifest schema. Add `artagents author compile` to write the JSON manifest into `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` (gitignored); the runtime hashes/pins this artifact. Add `artagents author check` (sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested non-trivial produces have semantic checks, code argv to `artagents orchestrators run` is rejected). Add `artagents author describe` (pretty-prints DAG: step kinds, produces, requires, max_iterations, repeat config, estimated cost ceiling if cost hints present). Add `artagents author new <pack>.<name>` (scaffolds Python file + fixtures/<name>/ + golden/<name>.events.jsonl from a template). The verify helper library (artagents/verify/) is already shipped from Phase 3 — re-use file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of as DSL helpers. Phases 1 (kernel), 2 (three step kinds), 3 (produces + repeat) are committed and shipped — build on top. Do NOT implement new lifecycle verbs (Phase 5). Keep additive: existing JSON manifests and python/command runtimes still work. Tests: typed step reference catches typos at definition; compile produces a valid manifest in build/; check validates references and rejects sentinel-only attested produces; describe pretty-prints expected DAG; new scaffolds correct structure; existing JSON manifests still load and run.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

``_PlanBuilder.to_dict()`` (which\nround-trips through ``artagents.core.task.plan.load_plan``), and writes the\nmanifest to ``<pack-root>/build/<name>.json`` as deterministic JSON.\n\nInline expansion of nested string-form refs (``nested(plan=\"<pack>.<name>\")``)\nruns during ``to_dict()``: ``compile_to_path`` passes itself in as the\n``_resolver`` and threads a ``_visiting`` set keyed on qualified id so a\nself- or mutually-recursive reference raises ``OrchestrateDefinitionError``\ninstead of recursing forever (FLAG-005).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport importlib.util\nimport json\nimport sys\nimport uuid\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom artagents._paths import REPO_ROOT\n\nfrom .dsl import OrchestrateDefinitionError, _PlanBuilder\n\nDEFAULT_PACKS_ROOT = REPO_ROOT / \"artagents\" / \"packs\"\n\n\ndef _qualified_split(qualified_id: str) -> tuple[str, str]:\n    if not isinstance(qualified_id, str) or not qualified_id:\n        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n            \"qualified id must be a non-empty string of the form '<pack>.<name>'\"\n        )\n    if \".\" not in qualified_id:\n        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n            f\"qualified id {qualified_id!r} must be '<pack>.<name>'\"\n        )\n    pack, _, name = qualified_id.partition(\".\")\n    if not pack or not name or \".\" in name:\n        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n            f\"qualified id {qualified_id!r} must be exactly '<pack>.<name>' \"\n            \"(no extra dots)\"\n        )\n    return pack, name\n\n\ndef _load_module_isolated(module_path: Path, qualified_id: str):\n    unique = f\"_artagents_orchestrate_{qualified_id.replace('.', '_')}_{uuid.uuid4().hex}\"\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(unique, module_path)\n    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:\n        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n            f\"could not load orchestrator module at {module_path}\"\n        )\n    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    sys.modules[unique] = module\n    try:\n        spec.loader.exec_module(module)\n    except Exception as exc:\n        sys.modules.pop(unique, None)\n        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n            f\"failed to import orchestrator module {module_path}: {exc}\"\n        ) from exc\n    finally:\n        sys.modules.pop(unique, None)\n    return module\n\n\ndef resolve_orchestrator(\n    qualified_id: str,\n    *,\n    packs_root: Optional[Path] = None,\n    _visiting: Optional[set] = None,\n) -> _PlanBuilder:\n    pack, name = _qualified_split(qualified_id)\n    root = Path(packs_root) if packs_root is not None else DEFAULT_PACKS_ROOT\n    pack_root = root / pack\n    if not pack_root.is_dir():\n        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n            f\"orchestrator {qualified_id!r}: pack directory not found at {pack_root}\"\n        )\n    module_path = pack_root / f\"{name}.py\"\n    if not module_path.is_file():\n        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n            f\"orchestrator {qualified_id!r}: module file not found at {module_path}\"\n        )\n    module = _load_module_isolated(module_path, qualified_id)\n\n    builders: list[_PlanBuilder] = [\n        value\n        for value in vars(module).values()\n        if isinstance(value, _PlanBuilder)\n    ]\n    if not builders:\n        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n            f\"orchestrator {qualified_id!r}: module {module_path} defines no \"\n            \"_PlanBuilder (use plan(...) or @orchestrator(...))\"\n        )\n    matching = [b for b in builders if b.plan_id == qualified_id]\n    if len(matching) == 1:\n        return matching[0]\n    if len(matching) > 1:\n        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n            f\"orchestrator {qualified_id!r}: module {module_path} defines \"\n            f\"multiple plans with plan_id {qualified_id!r}\"\n        )\n    if len(builders) == 1:\n        return builders[0]\n    ids = sorted({b.plan_id for b in builders})\n    raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(\n        f\"orchestrator {qualified_id!r}: module {module_path} defines multiple \"\n        f\"plans {ids}; declare exactly one with plan_id={qualified_id!r}\"\n    )\n\n\ndef _resolver_for(packs_root: Optional[Path]):\n    def _resolve(qualified_id: str, *, _visiting: Optional[set] = None) -> _PlanBuilder:\n        return resolve_orchestrator(\n            qualified_id, packs_root=packs_root, _visiting=_visiting\n        )\n\n    return _resolve\n\n\ndef compile_to_path(\n    qualified_id: str,\n    *,\n    dest: Optional[Path] = None,\n    packs_root: Optional[Path] = None,\n) -> Path:\n    pack, name = _qualified_split(qualified_id)\n    builder = resolve_orchestrator(qualified_id, packs_root=packs_root)\n    payload = builder.to_dict(_resolver=_resolver_for(packs_root))\n    root = Path(packs_root) if packs_root is not None else DEFAULT_PACKS_ROOT\n    if dest is not None:\n        out_path = Path(dest)\n    else:\n        out_path = root / pack / \"build\" / f\"{name}.json\"\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path.write_text(\n        json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + \"\\n\",\n        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n    )\n    return out_path\n"
}

pytest.raises(OrchestrateDefinitionError) as excinfo:\n            code(\n                \"bad\",\n                argv=[\"python3\", \"-m\", \"artagents\", \"orchestrators\", \"run\",
